id,english,transliteration,nepali
"0","I wake up early.","ma cāṃḍai uṭhchu.","म चाँडै उठ्छु।"
"1","You wake up late.","timī ḍhilo uṭhchau.","तिमी ढिलो उठ्छौ।"
"2","He wakes up at six.","ū cha baje uṭhcha.","ऊ छ बजे उठ्छ।"
"3","She wakes up at seven.","unī sāt baje uṭhchin.","उनी सात बजे उठ्छिन्।"
"4","We wake up together.","hāmī saṃgai uṭhchauṃ.","हामी सँगै उठ्छौं।"
"5","They wake up at dawn.","tinīharū bihān saberai uṭhchan.","तिनीहरू बिहान सबेरै उठ्छन्।"
"6","I brush my teeth every morning.","ma harek bihān dāṃt mājhchu.","म हरेक बिहान दाँत माझ्छु।"
"7","She brushes her teeth after breakfast.","unī brekaphāsṭa pachi dāṃt mājhchin.","उनी ब्रेकफास्ट पछि दाँत माझ्छिन्।"
"8","He takes a shower before work.","kām garnuaghi ū nuhāuṃch.","काम गर्नुअघि ऊ नुहाउँछ।"
"9","We eat breakfast together.","hāmī saṃgai brekaphāsṭa khānchauṃ.","हामी सँगै ब्रेकफास्ट खान्छौं।"
"10","I go to work every day.","ma harek din kāmamā jānchu.","म हरेक दिन काममा जान्छु।"
"11","She goes to school by bus.","unī basabāṭ skūl jānchin.","उनी बसबाट स्कूल जान्छिन्।"
"12","He walks to the office.","ū aphisatir hiṃḍcha.","ऊ अफिसतिर हिँड्छ।"
"13","They drive to work.","unīharū kāmamā jān gāḍī calāuṃchan.","उनीहरू काममा जान गाडी चलाउँछन्।"
"14","We take the train to the city.","hāmī śahar jāne rel caḍhchauṃ.","हामी शहर जाने रेल चढ्छौं।"
"15","I come home at five.","ma pāṃc baje ghar āuṃchu.","म पाँच बजे घर आउँछु।"
"16","She comes home late.","unī ḍhilo ghar āuṃchin.","उनी ढिलो घर आउँछिन्।"
"17","He arrives at the office at eight.","ū āṭh baje kāryālay āipugcha.","ऊ आठ बजे कार्यालय आइपुग्छ।"
"18","I leave the house at seven.","ma sāt baje gharabāṭ niskanchu.","म सात बजे घरबाट निस्कन्छु।"
"19","We leave for work together.","hāmī saṃgai kāmako lāgi niskanchauṃ.","हामी सँगै कामको लागि निस्कन्छौं।"
"20","I do not wake up early.","ma cāṃḍai uṭhdin.","म चाँडै उठ्दिन।"
"21","She does not take the bus.","unī bas caḍhdinan.","उनी बस चढ्दिनन्।"
"22","He does not eat breakfast.","ū bihānako khājā khāṃdain.","ऊ बिहानको खाजा खाँदैन।"
"23","We do not drive to work.","hāmī kāmamā gāḍī calāuṃdainauṃ.","हामी काममा गाडी चलाउँदैनौं।"
"24","They do not walk to school.","unīharū skūl hiṃḍer jāṃdainan.","उनीहरू स्कूल हिँडेर जाँदैनन्।"
"25","Do you wake up early?","ke tapāīṃ cāṃḍai uṭhnuhuncha?","के तपाईं चाँडै उठ्नुहुन्छ?"
"26","Does he take a shower?","ke usale nuhāuṃch?","के उसले नुहाउँछ?"
"27","Does she eat breakfast?","ke unī brekaphāsṭa khānchin?","के उनी ब्रेकफास्ट खान्छिन्?"
"28","What time do you wake up?","timī kati baje uṭhchau?","तिमी कति बजे उठ्छौ?"
"29","Where do you work?","tapāīṃ kahāṃ kām garnuhuncha?","तपाईं कहाँ काम गर्नुहुन्छ?"
"30","How do you get to work?","tapāīṃ kasarī kāmamā jānuhuncha?","तपाईं कसरी काममा जानुहुन्छ?"
"31","When does he come home?","ū kahile ghar āuṃch?","ऊ कहिले घर आउँछ?"
"32","Why do they leave early?","tinīharū kin cāṃḍai jānchan?","तिनीहरू किन चाँडै जान्छन्?"
"33","I woke up early yesterday.","ma hijo cāṃḍai uṭheṃ.","म हिजो चाँडै उठेँ।"
"34","She ate breakfast this morning.","unale āj bihān brekaphāsṭa khāin.","उनले आज बिहान ब्रेकफास्ट खाइन्।"
"35","He walked to work yesterday.","unī hijo kāmamā hiṃḍer gae.","उनी हिजो काममा हिँडेर गए।"
"36","We took the bus last week.","hāmīle gat haptā bas caḍhyauṃ.","हामीले गत हप्ता बस चढ्यौं।"
"37","They drove to the city.","unīharū śaharatir gāḍī calāer gae.","उनीहरू शहरतिर गाडी चलाएर गए।"
"38","I did not eat breakfast.","maile bihānako khājā khāinaṃ.","मैले बिहानको खाजा खाइनँ।"
"39","She did not go to work.","unī kāmamā gainan.","उनी काममा गइनन्।"
"40","Did you wake up early?","ke tapāīṃ cāṃḍai uṭhnubhayo?","के तपाईं चाँडै उठ्नुभयो?"
"41","Did he come home late?","ke ū ḍhilo ghar āyo?","के ऊ ढिलो घर आयो?"
"42","What did you eat for breakfast?","tapāīṃle bihānako khājāmā ke khānubhayo?","तपाईंले बिहानको खाजामा के खानुभयो?"
"43","I will wake up early tomorrow.","ma bholi cāṃḍai uṭhnechu.","म भोलि चाँडै उठ्नेछु।"
"44","She will take the bus.","unī bas linechin.","उनी बस लिनेछिन्।"
"45","He will walk to work.","ū kāmamā hiṃḍer jānech.","ऊ काममा हिँडेर जानेछ।"
"46","We will eat dinner together.","hāmī saṃgai khānā khānechauṃ.","हामी सँगै खाना खानेछौं।"
"47","They will come home late.","tinīharū ḍhilo ghar āunechan.","तिनीहरू ढिलो घर आउनेछन्।"
"48","I will not go to work tomorrow.","ma bholi kāmamā jāṃdin.","म भोलि काममा जाँदिन।"
"49","Will you eat breakfast?","ke tapāīṃ bihānako khājā khānuhuncha?","के तपाईं बिहानको खाजा खानुहुन्छ?"
"50","When will she come home?","unī kahile ghar āunechin?","उनी कहिले घर आउनेछिन्?"
"51","I am eating breakfast.","ma brekaphāsṭa khāṃdai chu.","म ब्रेकफास्ट खाँदै छु।"
"52","She is walking to school.","unī skūl hiṃḍer jāṃdaichin.","उनी स्कूल हिँडेर जाँदैछिन्।"
"53","He is taking a shower.","ū nuhāuṃdai cha.","ऊ नुहाउँदै छ।"
"54","We are getting ready.","hāmī tayārī gardaichauṃ.","हामी तयारी गर्दैछौं।"
"55","They are leaving now.","unīharū ahile jāṃdaichan.","उनीहरू अहिले जाँदैछन्।"
"56","I was eating when you called.","timīle phon gardā ma khānā khāiraheko thieṃ.","तिमीले फोन गर्दा म खाना खाइरहेको थिएँ।"
"57","She was sleeping when I arrived.","ma āipugdā unī sutirahekī thiin.","म आइपुग्दा उनी सुतिरहेकी थिइन्।"
"58","I have already eaten.","maile pahile nai khāisakeko chu.","मैले पहिले नै खाइसकेको छु।"
"59","She has already left.","unī pahile nai gaisakekī chin.","उनी पहिले नै गइसकेकी छिन्।"
"60","He has not eaten yet.","usale ahilesamma khānā khāeko chain.","उसले अहिलेसम्म खाना खाएको छैन।"
"61","We have been here since morning.","hāmī bihānaidekhi yahāṃ chauṃ.","हामी बिहानैदेखि यहाँ छौं।"
"62","They had already left when I arrived.","ma āipugdā unīharū pahile nai gaisakekā thie.","म आइपुग्दा उनीहरू पहिले नै गइसकेका थिए।"
"63","Wake up!","uṭhnuhos!","उठ्नुहोस्!"
"64","Do not be late!","ḍhilo nagarnuhos!","ढिलो नगर्नुहोस्!"
"65","Eat your breakfast.","bihānako khājā khānuhos.","बिहानको खाजा खानुहोस्।"
"66","Go to bed early.","cāṃḍai sutnuhos.","चाँडै सुत्नुहोस्।"
"67","Let us leave now.","ab hāmī jāauṃ.","अब हामी जाऔं।"
"68","I can wake up early.","ma cāṃḍai uṭhna sakchu.","म चाँडै उठ्न सक्छु।"
"69","She must go to work.","unī kāmamā jānai parcha.","उनी काममा जानै पर्छ।"
"70","He should eat breakfast.","usale brekaphāsṭa khānuparcha.","उसले ब्रेकफास्ट खानुपर्छ।"
"71","We need to leave now.","hāmī ab jānu parcha.","हामी अब जानु पर्छ।"
"72","You may stay home today.","tapāīṃ āj gharamai basna saknuhuncha.","तपाईं आज घरमै बस्न सक्नुहुन्छ।"
"73","Can you help me?","ke tapāīṃ malāī maddat garna saknuhuncha?","के तपाईं मलाई मद्दत गर्न सक्नुहुन्छ?"
"74","I cannot find my keys.","ma merā sāṃcoharū phelā pārna sakdin.","म मेरा साँचोहरू फेला पार्न सक्दिन।"
"75","Breakfast is prepared by my mother.","bihānako khājā merī āmāle banāunuhuncha.","बिहानको खाजा मेरी आमाले बनाउनुहुन्छ।"
"76","The house was cleaned yesterday.","ghar hijo saphā garieko thiyo.","घर हिजो सफा गरिएको थियो।"
"77","Dinner will be cooked by him.","khānā usaile pakāunech.","खाना उसैले पकाउनेछ।"
"78","If I wake up early, I will exercise.","yadi ma cāṃḍai uṭhe bhane, ma vyāyām garnechu.","यदि म चाँडै उठे भने, म व्यायाम गर्नेछु।"
"79","If she had time, she would cook breakfast.","yadi unīsaṃg samay thiyo bhane, unī brekaphāsṭa pakāuṃchin.","यदि उनीसँग समय थियो भने, उनी ब्रेकफास्ट पकाउँछिन्।"
"80","If I had woken up earlier, I would not have been late.","yadi ma pahile nai uṭheko bhae, ma ḍhilo hune thiinaṃ.","यदि म पहिले नै उठेको भए, म ढिलो हुने थिइनँ।"
"81","I wake up and I eat breakfast.","ma uṭhchu ra nāstā khānchu.","म उठ्छु र नास्ता खान्छु।"
"82","He works but she studies.","ū kām garche tar unī paḍhchin.","ऊ काम गर्छे तर उनी पढ्छिन्।"
"83","I eat breakfast because I am hungry.","ma bhokāekole bihānako khājā khānchu.","म भोकाएकोले बिहानको खाजा खान्छु।"
"84","She brushes her teeth before she leaves.","unī jānuaghi dāṃt mājhchin.","उनी जानुअघि दाँत माझ्छिन्।"
"85","Although I am tired, I go to work.","thākeko bhae pani, ma kāmamā jānchu.","थाकेको भए पनि, म काममा जान्छु।"
"86","This is my house.","yo mero ghar ho.","यो मेरो घर हो।"
"87","That is her car.","tyo unako gāḍī ho.","त्यो उनको गाडी हो।"
"88","I dress myself.","ma āphaiṃ lugā lagāuṃchu.","म आफैँ लुगा लगाउँछु।"
"89","He shaves himself every morning.","unī harek bihān āphno dāhrī khauranchan.","उनी हरेक बिहान आफ्नो दाह्री खौरन्छन्।"
"90","I wake up earlier than my brother.","ma mero bhāi bhandā pahile uṭhchu.","म मेरो भाइ भन्दा पहिले उठ्छु।"
"91","She is the fastest runner in our family.","unī hāmro parivāramā sabaibhandā chiṭo dhāvak hun.","उनी हाम्रो परिवारमा सबैभन्दा छिटो धावक हुन्।"
"92","Today is busier than yesterday.","āj hijo bhandā baḍhī vyasta cha.","आज हिजो भन्दा बढी व्यस्त छ।"
"93","There is a park near my house.","mero ghar najikai euṭā pārka cha.","मेरो घर नजिकै एउटा पार्क छ।"
"94","There are many people on the street.","saḍakamā dherai mānisaharū chan.","सडकमा धेरै मानिसहरू छन्।"
"95","Everyone goes to work.","sabaijanā kāmamā jānchan.","सबैजना काममा जान्छन्।"
"96","Nobody stays home during the week.","haptābhari kohī pani gharamā basdainan.","हप्ताभरि कोही पनि घरमा बस्दैनन्।"
"97","Some people wake up very early.","katipay mānisaharū dherai cāṃḍai uṭhchan.","कतिपय मानिसहरू धेरै चाँडै उठ्छन्।"
"98","The man who lives next door is a teacher.","cheumā basne mānche śikṣak ho.","छेउमा बस्ने मान्छे शिक्षक हो।"
"99","The bus that I take is always late.","ma caḍhne bas sadhaiṃ ḍhilo huncha.","म चढ्ने बस सधैं ढिलो हुन्छ।"
"100","The house where I grew up is very old.","ma hurkeko ghar dherai purāno cha.","म हुर्केको घर धेरै पुरानो छ।"
"101","I always eat lunch at noon.","ma sadhaiṃ diuṃso khānā khānchu.","म सधैं दिउँसो खाना खान्छु।"
"102","She sometimes walks to work.","unī kahilekāhīṃ kāmamā hiṃḍer jānchin.","उनी कहिलेकाहीँ काममा हिँडेर जान्छिन्।"
"103","He never eats dinner late.","unī kahilyai ḍhilo khānā khāndainan.","उनी कहिल्यै ढिलो खाना खान्दैनन्।"
"104","We usually go to bed at ten.","hāmī sāmānyatayā das baje sutchauṃ.","हामी सामान्यतया दस बजे सुत्छौं।"
"105","I brush my teeth twice a day.","ma dinamā duī paṭak dāṃt mājhchu.","म दिनमा दुई पटक दाँत माझ्छु।"
"106","The keys are on the table.","sāṃcoharū ṭebalamā chan.","साँचोहरू टेबलमा छन्।"
"107","My phone is in my bag.","mero phon mero jholāmā cha.","मेरो फोन मेरो झोलामा छ।"
"108","The car is in front of the house.","gāḍī gharako agāḍi cha.","गाडी घरको अगाडि छ।"
"109","The park is behind the school.","pārka skūlako pachāḍi cha.","पार्क स्कूलको पछाडि छ।"
"110","I sit next to my colleague.","ma mero sahakarmīko cheumā baschu.","म मेरो सहकर्मीको छेउमा बस्छु।"
"111","He said that he was tired.","usale bhanyo ki ū thākeko thiyo.","उसले भन्यो कि ऊ थाकेको थियो।"
"112","She told me that she would be late.","unale malāī ḍhilo hune batāin.","उनले मलाई ढिलो हुने बताइन्।"
"113","They asked if I was coming.","unīharūle ma āuṃdaichu ki bhaner sodhe.","उनीहरूले म आउँदैछु कि भनेर सोधे।"
"114","I clean the house on Saturdays.","ma śanibār ghar saphā garchu.","म शनिबार घर सफा गर्छु।"
"115","She does the laundry every week.","unī harek haptā lugā dhunchin.","उनी हरेक हप्ता लुगा धुन्छिन्।"
"116","He cooks dinner for the family.","unī parivārako lāgi khānā pakāuṃchan.","उनी परिवारको लागि खाना पकाउँछन्।"
"117","We watch television in the evening.","hāmī sāṃjh ṭelibhijan herchauṃ.","हामी साँझ टेलिभिजन हेर्छौं।"
"118","They play in the garden after school.","unīharū skūl pachi bagaiṃcāmā khelchan.","उनीहरू स्कूल पछि बगैंचामा खेल्छन्।"
"119","I read a book before bed.","sutnuaghi ma euṭā kitāb paḍhchu.","सुत्नुअघि म एउटा किताब पढ्छु।"
"120","She listens to music while cooking.","khānā pakāuṃdā unī saṃgīt sunchin.","खाना पकाउँदा उनी संगीत सुन्छिन्।"
"121","He takes the dog for a walk.","usale kukuralāī ghumāun laijāncha.","उसले कुकुरलाई घुमाउन लैजान्छ।"
"122","I drink coffee every morning.","ma harek bihān kaphī piuṃchu.","म हरेक बिहान कफी पिउँछु।"
"123","The children play outside.","keṭākeṭīharū bāhir khelchan.","केटाकेटीहरू बाहिर खेल्छन्।"
"124","My mother cooks the best food.","merī āmāle sabaibhandā rāmro khānā pakāunuhuncha.","मेरी आमाले सबैभन्दा राम्रो खाना पकाउनुहुन्छ।"
"125","Our neighbor is very friendly.","hāmro chimekī dherai milanasār cha.","हाम्रो छिमेकी धेरै मिलनसार छ।"
"126","I need to buy groceries.","malāī kirānā sāmān kinnuparnech.","मलाई किराना सामान किन्नुपर्नेछ।"
"127","She forgot her umbrella.","unale āphno chātā birsin.","उनले आफ्नो छाता बिर्सिन्।"
"128","The alarm clock rings at six.","cha baje alārma ghaḍī bajcha.","छ बजे अलार्म घडी बज्छ।"
"129","I usually take a nap after lunch.","ma prāyaḥ diuṃsoko khānā pachi ekachin sutchu.","म प्रायः दिउँसोको खाना पछि एकछिन सुत्छु।"
"130","He is getting dressed.","ū lugā lagāuṃdai cha.","ऊ लुगा लगाउँदै छ।"
"131","I have been living here for five years.","ma yahāṃ pāṃc varṣadekhi basdai āeko chu.","म यहाँ पाँच वर्षदेखि बस्दै आएको छु।"
"132","She has just arrived.","unī bharkharai āipugekī chin.","उनी भर्खरै आइपुगेकी छिन्।"
"133","Where are my shoes?","merā juttāharū kahāṃ chan?","मेरा जुत्ताहरू कहाँ छन्?"
"134","How many rooms does your house have?","tapāīṃko gharamā kativaṭā koṭhā chan?","तपाईंको घरमा कतिवटा कोठा छन्?"
"135","I wish I could sleep more.","ma ajhai sutna saktheṃ.","म अझै सुत्न सक्थेँ।"
"136","If it rains, I will stay home.","yadi pānī paryo bhane, ma gharamai basnechu.","यदि पानी पर्यो भने, म घरमै बस्नेछु।"
"137","Let me help you.","malāī maddat garna dinuhos.","मलाई मद्दत गर्न दिनुहोस्।"
"138","The door was left open.","ḍhokā khulā nai choḍiyo.","ढोका खुला नै छोडियो।"
"139","Someone knocked on the door.","kasaile ḍhokā ḍhakaḍhakyāyo.","कसैले ढोका ढकढक्यायो।"
"140","I like rice.","malāī bhāt man parcha.","मलाई भात मन पर्छ।"
"141","She likes spicy food.","unalāī masalādār khānā man parcha.","उनलाई मसलादार खाना मन पर्छ।"
"142","He eats bread every day.","unī harek din roṭī khānchan.","उनी हरेक दिन रोटी खान्छन्।"
"143","We drink tea in the afternoon.","hāmī diuṃso ciyā piuṃchauṃ.","हामी दिउँसो चिया पिउँछौं।"
"144","They prefer coffee to tea.","unīharū ciyābhandā kaphī man parāuṃchan.","उनीहरू चियाभन्दा कफी मन पराउँछन्।"
"145","The soup is hot.","sup tāto cha.","सुप तातो छ।"
"146","The milk is cold.","dūdh ciso cha.","दूध चिसो छ।"
"147","The bread is fresh.","roṭī tājā cha.","रोटी ताजा छ।"
"148","The fruit is sweet.","phal guliyo cha.","फल गुलियो छ।"
"149","This food tastes delicious.","yo khānā svādiṣṭa lāgcha.","यो खाना स्वादिष्ट लाग्छ।"
"150","I cook dinner every evening.","ma harek sāṃjh khānā pakāuṃchu.","म हरेक साँझ खाना पकाउँछु।"
"151","She is cooking lunch.","unī khānā pakāuṃdai chin.","उनी खाना पकाउँदै छिन्।"
"152","He boils water for tea.","usale ciyāko lāgi pānī umālcha.","उसले चियाको लागि पानी उमाल्छ।"
"153","We bake bread on Sundays.","hāmī āitabār roṭī pakāuṃchauṃ.","हामी आइतबार रोटी पकाउँछौं।"
"154","They fry the fish in oil.","tinīharūle māchālāī telamā phrāi garchan.","तिनीहरूले माछालाई तेलमा फ्राइ गर्छन्।"
"155","I cut the vegetables.","maile tarakārī kāṭeṃ.","मैले तरकारी काटें।"
"156","She stirs the sauce.","unī caṭanī calāuṃchin.","उनी चटनी चलाउँछिन्।"
"157","He adds salt to the soup.","usale supamā nun thapcha.","उसले सुपमा नुन थप्छ।"
"158","We mix the ingredients together.","hāmī sāmagrīharū saṃgai milāuṃchauṃ.","हामी सामग्रीहरू सँगै मिलाउँछौं।"
"159","The chef prepares the meal.","sephale khānā tayār garcha.","सेफले खाना तयार गर्छ।"
"160","I do not eat meat.","ma māsu khāṃdin.","म मासु खाँदिन।"
"161","She does not like onions.","usalāī pyāj man pardain.","उसलाई प्याज मन पर्दैन।"
"162","He never drinks alcohol.","ū kahilyai raksī piuṃdain.","ऊ कहिल्यै रक्सी पिउँदैन।"
"163","We do not add sugar.","hāmī cinī thapdainauṃ.","हामी चिनी थप्दैनौं।"
"164","The water is not boiling yet.","pānī ajhai umliraheko chain.","पानी अझै उम्लिरहेको छैन।"
"165","Do you like fish?","ke tapāīṃlāī māchā man parcha?","के तपाईंलाई माछा मन पर्छ?"

"174","She made a cake for the party.","unale pārṭīko lāgi kek banāin.","उनले पार्टीको लागि केक बनाइन्।"
"175","He ate too much.","usale dherai khāyo.","उसले धेरै खायो।"
"176","We had dinner at a restaurant.","hāmīle euṭā resṭurenṭamā khānā khāyauṃ.","हामीले एउटा रेस्टुरेन्टमा खाना खायौं।"
"177","They brought wine to the dinner.","unīharūle khānāmā vāin lyāe.","उनीहरूले खानामा वाइन ल्याए।"
"178","I did not finish my meal.","maile khānā khāeko thiinaṃ.","मैले खाना खाएको थिइनँ।"
"179","The soup was too salty.","sup dherai nunilo thiyo.","सुप धेरै नुनिलो थियो।"
"180","Did you enjoy the food?","ke tapāīṃle khānāko ānanda linubhayo?","के तपाईंले खानाको आनन्द लिनुभयो?"
"181","What did she cook?","unale ke pakāin?","उनले के पकाइन्?"
"182","I will cook dinner tonight.","ma āj rātī khānā pakāuṃchu.","म आज राती खाना पकाउँछु।"

"184","We will eat at a restaurant.","hāmī resṭurenṭamā khānā khānechauṃ.","हामी रेस्टुरेन्टमा खाना खानेछौं।"
"185","Will you try this dish?","ke tapāīṃ yo parikār prayās garnuhuncha?","के तपाईं यो परिकार प्रयास गर्नुहुन्छ?"
"186","I am going to cook fish.","ma māchā pakāun jāṃdaichu.","म माछा पकाउन जाँदैछु।"
"187","She has never tasted this fruit.","unale yo phal kahilyai cākhekī chainan.","उनले यो फल कहिल्यै चाखेकी छैनन्।"
"188","We have been cooking for two hours.","hāmī duī ghaṇṭādekhi khānā pakāuṃdai chauṃ.","हामी दुई घण्टादेखि खाना पकाउँदै छौं।"
"189","The food has gotten cold.","khānā ciso bhaisakeko cha.","खाना चिसो भइसकेको छ।"
"190","He had already eaten before we arrived.","hāmī āipugnubhandā pahile nai usale khānā khāisakeko thiyo.","हामी आइपुग्नुभन्दा पहिले नै उसले खाना खाइसकेको थियो।"
"191","The bread is baked in a wood oven.","roṭī kāṭhako culhomā pakāincha.","रोटी काठको चुल्होमा पकाइन्छ।"
"192","The fish was cooked by my grandmother.","māchā merī hajuraāmāle pakāunubhaeko thiyo.","माछा मेरी हजुरआमाले पकाउनुभएको थियो।"
"193","The vegetables are washed before cooking.","tarakārīharū pakāunu aghi dhoincha.","तरकारीहरू पकाउनु अघि धोइन्छ।"

"195","The cake will be finished soon.","kek cāṃḍai nai tayār hunech.","केक चाँडै नै तयार हुनेछ।"

"197","Do not burn the food!","khānā najalāunuhos!","खाना नजलाउनुहोस्!"
"198","Add a little salt.","thorai nun thapnuhos.","थोरै नुन थप्नुहोस्।"
"199","Taste this and tell me what you think.","yo cākhnuhos ra tapāīṃlāī kasto lāgcha malāī bhannuhos.","यो चाख्नुहोस् र तपाईंलाई कस्तो लाग्छ मलाई भन्नुहोस्।"
"200","Let us order dessert.","miṭhāī arḍar garauṃ.","मिठाई अर्डर गरौं।"
"201","If you add too much salt, the food will be ruined.","yadi tapāīṃle dherai nun thapnubhayo bhane, khānā bigrancha.","यदि तपाईंले धेरै नुन थप्नुभयो भने, खाना बिग्रन्छ।"
"202","If I had more time, I would bake a cake.","yadi masaṃg dherai samay bhaeko bhae, ma kek banāutheṃ.","यदि मसँग धेरै समय भएको भए, म केक बनाउथें।"
"203","If she had bought the ingredients, we would have cooked.","yadi unale sāmagrīharū kineko bhae, hāmīle khānā pakāune thiyauṃ.","यदि उनले सामग्रीहरू किनेको भए, हामीले खाना पकाउने थियौं।"
"204","You should eat more vegetables.","tapāīṃle dherai tarakārī khānuparcha.","तपाईंले धेरै तरकारी खानुपर्छ।"
"205","We must wash the fruit before eating.","hāmīle phalaphūl khānuaghi dhunuparcha.","हामीले फलफूल खानुअघि धुनुपर्छ।"
"206","I can cook Italian food.","ma iṭāliyan khānā pakāun sakchu.","म इटालियन खाना पकाउन सक्छु।"

"212","The freshest fish is at the morning market.","sabaibhandā tājā māchā bihānako bajāramā pāincha.","सबैभन्दा ताजा माछा बिहानको बजारमा पाइन्छ।"
"213","I want some water.","malāī pānī cāhiyo.","मलाई पानी चाहियो।"

"219","The restaurant where we ate last night is famous.","hāmīle hijo rātī khāeko resṭurenṭa prasiddha cha.","हामीले हिजो राती खाएको रेस्टुरेन्ट प्रसिद्ध छ।"
"220","The chef who trained in Paris cooks the best food.","perisamā tālim liekā śephale sabaibhandā rāmro khānā pakāuṃchan.","पेरिसमा तालिम लिएका शेफले सबैभन्दा राम्रो खाना पकाउँछन्।"

"223","He eats a lot because he is always hungry.","ū dherai khāncha kinabhane ū sadhaiṃ bhoko huncha.","ऊ धेरै खान्छ किनभने ऊ सधैं भोको हुन्छ।"
"224","I will cook dinner or we can order food.","ma khānā pakāuṃchu vā hāmī khānā arḍar garna sakchauṃ.","म खाना पकाउँछु वा हामी खाना अर्डर गर्न सक्छौं।"
"225","Although the food was expensive, it was delicious.","khānā mahaṃgo bhae pani svādiṣṭa thiyo.","खाना महँगो भए पनि स्वादिष्ट थियो।"

"265","Did you enjoy your trip?","ke tapāīṃle āphno yātrāko ānanda linubhayo?","के तपाईंले आफ्नो यात्राको आनन्द लिनुभयो?"
"266","Where did you go on vacation?","tapāīṃ bidāmā kahāṃ jānubhayo?","तपाईं बिदामा कहाँ जानुभयो?"

"268","She is going to visit her family.","unī āphno parivāralāī bheṭna jāṃdaichin.","उनी आफ्नो परिवारलाई भेट्न जाँदैछिन्।"
"269","We will take the train.","hāmī rel linechauṃ.","हामी रेल लिनेछौं।"

"274","She is packing her bags.","unī āphno jholā pyāk gardaichin.","उनी आफ्नो झोला प्याक गर्दैछिन्।"
"275","They are boarding the plane.","unīharū vimān caḍhdai chan.","उनीहरू विमान चढ्दै छन्।"
"276","We were driving when it started to rain.","pānī parna thāldā hāmī gāḍī calāirahekā thiyauṃ.","पानी पर्न थाल्दा हामी गाडी चलाइरहेका थियौं।"
"277","I have been to Italy three times.","ma tīn paṭak iṭālī gaeko chu.","म तीन पटक इटाली गएको छु।"
"278","She has never flown before.","unī pahile kahilyai uḍekī chainan.","उनी पहिले कहिल्यै उडेकी छैनन्।"
"279","We have already booked the hotel.","hāmīle pahile nai hoṭal buk garisakekā chauṃ.","हामीले पहिले नै होटल बुक गरिसकेका छौं।"
"280","They had already left when we arrived.","hāmī āipugdā unīharū pahile nai gaisakekā thie.","हामी आइपुग्दा उनीहरू पहिले नै गइसकेका थिए।"
"281","He has been traveling for three months.","unī tīn mahinādekhi yātrā gardai āekā chan.","उनी तीन महिनादेखि यात्रा गर्दै आएका छन्।"
"282","The ticket was bought online.","ṭikaṭ analāinabāṭ kinieko thiyo.","टिकट अनलाइनबाट किनिएको थियो।"
"283","The flight was canceled.","uḍān radda gariyo.","उडान रद्द गरियो।"
"284","Passengers are asked to show their passports.","yātruharūlāī āphno rāhadānī dekhāun bhanieko cha.","यात्रुहरूलाई आफ्नो राहदानी देखाउन भनिएको छ।"
"285","The hotel room has been reserved.","hoṭalako koṭhā rijarbha garieko cha.","होटलको कोठा रिजर्भ गरिएको छ।"
"286","Fasten your seatbelt.","āphno siṭabelṭa bāṃdhnuhos.","आफ्नो सिटबेल्ट बाँध्नुहोस्।"
"287","Do not leave your luggage unattended.","āphno sāmān bevāstā nagarnuhos.","आफ्नो सामान बेवास्ता नगर्नुहोस्।"
"288","Please show me your ticket.","kr̥payā malāī tapāīṃko ṭikaṭ dekhāunuhos.","कृपया मलाई तपाईंको टिकट देखाउनुहोस्।"
"289","If we leave now, we will catch the train.","yadi hāmī ahile niskiyauṃ bhane, hāmī rel samātnechauṃ.","यदि हामी अहिले निस्कियौं भने, हामी रेल समात्नेछौं।"
"290","If I had a car, I would drive there.","yadi mero gāḍī bhaeko bhae, ma tyahāṃ gāḍī calāune thieṃ.","यदि मेरो गाडी भएको भए, म त्यहाँ गाडी चलाउने थिएँ।"
"291","If they had arrived earlier, they would not have missed the flight.","yadi tinīharū pahile āipugekā bhae, tinīharūle uḍān chuṭāune thienan.","यदि तिनीहरू पहिले आइपुगेका भए, तिनीहरूले उडान छुटाउने थिएनन्।"
"292","You must arrive two hours before the flight.","tapāīṃ uḍān bhandā duī ghaṇṭā agāḍi āipugnu parcha.","तपाईं उडान भन्दा दुई घण्टा अगाडि आइपुग्नु पर्छ।"
"293","I should book the hotel in advance.","maile pahile nai hoṭal buk garnuparcha.","मैले पहिले नै होटल बुक गर्नुपर्छ।"
"294","We can take a taxi to the center.","hāmī kendramā ṭyāksī lin sakchauṃ.","हामी केन्द्रमा ट्याक्सी लिन सक्छौं।"
"295","She could not find her gate.","unale āphno ḍhokā phelā pārna sakinan.","उनले आफ्नो ढोका फेला पार्न सकिनन्।"
"296","The train is faster than the bus.","rel bas bhandā chiṭo cha.","रेल बस भन्दा छिटो छ।"
"297","Flying is the quickest way to travel.","uḍān yātrā garne sabaibhandā chiṭo tarikā ho.","उडान यात्रा गर्ने सबैभन्दा छिटो तरिका हो।"
"298","This hotel is more expensive than that one.","yo hoṭel tyo bhandā mahaṃgo cha.","यो होटेल त्यो भन्दा महँगो छ।"
"299","It was the longest flight I have ever taken.","yo maile lieko sabaibhandā lāmo uḍān thiyo.","यो मैले लिएको सबैभन्दा लामो उडान थियो।"
"300","The airport is far from the city.","vimānasthal śaharabāṭ dherai ṭāḍhā cha.","विमानस्थल शहरबाट धेरै टाढा छ।"
"301","The hotel is near the beach.","hoṭal samudra taṭ najikai cha.","होटल समुद्र तट नजिकै छ।"
"302","Go straight and turn left.","sidhā jānuhos ra bāyāṃ moḍnuhos.","सिधा जानुहोस् र बायाँ मोड्नुहोस्।"
"303","The station is across the street.","sṭeśan saḍak pār cha.","स्टेशन सडक पार छ।"
"304","The museum is between the park and the river.","saṃgrahālay pārka ra nadīko bīcamā cha.","संग्रहालय पार्क र नदीको बीचमा छ।"
"305","She said that the flight was delayed.","unale bhanin ki uḍān ḍhilo bhayo.","उनले भनिन् कि उडान ढिलो भयो।"
"306","He told me that the hotel was fully booked.","usale malāī bhanyo ki hoṭal pūrṇa rūpamā buk bhaisakeko thiyo.","उसले मलाई भन्यो कि होटल पूर्ण रूपमा बुक भइसकेको थियो।"
"307","They asked us where we were going.","unīharūle hāmīlāī kahāṃ jāṃdaich bhaner sodhe.","उनीहरूले हामीलाई कहाँ जाँदैछ भनेर सोधे।"
"308","All passengers must board now.","sabai yātruharū ab caḍhnuparcha.","सबै यात्रुहरू अब चढ्नुपर्छ।"
"309","Some flights were canceled.","kehī uḍānaharū radda gariyo.","केही उडानहरू रद्द गरियो।"
"310","No one was at the station.","sṭeśanamā kohī pani thienan.","स्टेशनमा कोही पनि थिएनन्।"
"311","I have not seen any taxis.","maile kunai ṭyāksī dekheko chain.","मैले कुनै ट्याक्सी देखेको छैन।"
"312","The city where we stayed was beautiful.","hāmī baseko śahar sundar thiyo.","हामी बसेको शहर सुन्दर थियो।"
"313","The woman who sat next to me on the plane was very kind.","vimānamā mero cheumā basekī mahilā dherai dayālu thiin.","विमानमा मेरो छेउमा बसेकी महिला धेरै दयालु थिइन्।"
"314","We can take the bus or walk.","hāmī bas caḍhna vā hiṃḍna sakchauṃ.","हामी बस चढ्न वा हिँड्न सक्छौं।"
"315","I want to travel, but I do not have enough money.","ma yātrā garna cāhanchu, tar masaṃg paryāpta paisā chain.","म यात्रा गर्न चाहन्छु, तर मसँग पर्याप्त पैसा छैन।"
"316","She travels a lot because she loves exploring new places.","unī dherai yātrā garchin kinabhane unalāī nayāṃ ṭhāuṃharū anveṣaṇ garna man parcha.","उनी धेरै यात्रा गर्छिन् किनभने उनलाई नयाँ ठाउँहरू अन्वेषण गर्न मन पर्छ।"
"317","Although the journey was long, we enjoyed it.","yātrā lāmo bhae pani hāmīle ramāilo garyauṃ.","यात्रा लामो भए पनि हामीले रमाइलो गर्यौं।"
"318","How long does the flight take?","uḍān kati samay lāgcha?","उडान कति समय लाग्छ?"
"319","I would like to rent a car.","ma gāḍī bhāḍāmā lin cāhanchu.","म गाडी भाडामा लिन चाहन्छु।"
"320","The taxi driver took us to the wrong address.","ṭyāksī cālakale hāmīlāī galat ṭhegānāmā puryāyo.","ट्याक्सी चालकले हामीलाई गलत ठेगानामा पुर्‍यायो।"
"321","We are lost.","hāmī harāyauṃ.","हामी हरायौं।"
"322","Can you tell me how to get to the train station?","rel sṭesan kasarī pugne bhaner batāun saknuhuncha?","रेल स्टेसन कसरी पुग्ने भनेर बताउन सक्नुहुन्छ?"
"323","I have never been here before.","ma yahāṃ pahile kahilyai āeko chain.","म यहाँ पहिले कहिल्यै आएको छैन।"
"324","Excuse me, where is the nearest bus stop?","māph garnuhos, najikako bas sṭap kahāṃ cha?","माफ गर्नुहोस्, नजिकको बस स्टप कहाँ छ?"
"325","My mother is a doctor.","merī āmā ḍākṭar hunuhuncha.","मेरी आमा डाक्टर हुनुहुन्छ।"
"326","His father works in a factory.","unakā bubā euṭā kārakhānāmā kām garchan.","उनका बुबा एउटा कारखानामा काम गर्छन्।"
"327","She has two brothers and one sister.","unakā duī bhāi ra ek bahinī chan.","उनका दुई भाइ र एक बहिनी छन्।"
"328","We live with our grandparents.","hāmī hāmrā hajurabā hajuraāmāsaṃg baschauṃ.","हामी हाम्रा हजुरबा हजुरआमासँग बस्छौं।"
"329","Their family is very large.","unīharūko parivār dherai ṭhūlo cha.","उनीहरूको परिवार धेरै ठूलो छ।"
"330","The baby is sleeping.","baccā sutiraheko cha.","बच्चा सुतिरहेको छ।"
"331","My oldest brother is married.","mero jeṭho bhāi vivāhit cha.","मेरो जेठो भाइ विवाहित छ।"
"332","Her younger sister studies at the university.","unakī kānchī bahinī viśvavidyālayamā paḍhchin.","उनकी कान्छी बहिनी विश्वविद्यालयमा पढ्छिन्।"
"333","I love my parents.","ma merā āmābābulāī māyā garchu.","म मेरा आमाबाबुलाई माया गर्छु।"
"334","He takes care of his grandmother.","usale hajuraāmāko heracāh garcha.","उसले हजुरआमाको हेरचाह गर्छ।"
"335","Our house has three bedrooms.","hāmro gharamā tīnavaṭā sutne koṭhā chan.","हाम्रो घरमा तीनवटा सुत्ने कोठा छन्।"
"336","The living room is next to the kitchen.","baiṭhak koṭhā bhānsākoṭhāko cheumā cha.","बैठक कोठा भान्साकोठाको छेउमा छ।"
"337","There is a garden behind the house.","ghar pachāḍi euṭā bagaiṃcā cha.","घर पछाडि एउटा बगैँचा छ।"
"338","The bathroom is upstairs.","bātharūm māthillo talāmā cha.","बाथरूम माथिल्लो तलामा छ।"
"339","We painted the walls last month.","hāmīle gat mahinā bhittāharū raṃgāyauṃ.","हामीले गत महिना भित्ताहरू रंगायौं।"
"340","She decorated her room.","unale āphno koṭhā sajāin.","उनले आफ्नो कोठा सजाइन्।"
"341","The roof needs to be repaired.","chānā marmat garnuparnech.","छाना मर्मत गर्नुपर्नेछ।"
"342","I want to buy a new sofa.","ma nayāṃ sophā kinna cāhanchu.","म नयाँ सोफा किन्न चाहन्छु।"
"343","He built a bookshelf himself.","unale āphaiṃle kitāb rākhne darāj banāe.","उनले आफैंले किताब राख्ने दराज बनाए।"
"344","The house was built fifty years ago.","ghar pacās varṣa pahile banāieko thiyo.","घर पचास वर्ष पहिले बनाइएको थियो।"
"345","I do not have any siblings.","mero kunai dājubhāi didībahinī chain.","मेरो कुनै दाजुभाइ दिदीबहिनी छैन।"
"346","She does not live with her parents.","unī āphnā āmābābusaṃg basdinan.","उनी आफ्ना आमाबाबुसँग बस्दिनन्।"
"347","He is not married.","unī vivāhit chainan.","उनी विवाहित छैनन्।"
"348","We have never moved to another city.","hāmī kahilyai arko śaharamā sarekā chainauṃ.","हामी कहिल्यै अर्को शहरमा सरेका छैनौं।"
"349","How many children do you have?","tapāīṃkā kati janā baccāharū chan?","तपाईंका कति जना बच्चाहरू छन्?"
"350","Where does your family live?","tapāīṃko parivār kahāṃ bascha?","तपाईंको परिवार कहाँ बस्छ?"
"351","Do you have any pets?","ke tapāīṃsaṃg kunai gharapāluvā janāvar cha?","के तपाईंसँग कुनै घरपालुवा जनावर छ?"
"352","Is your father at home?","timro bubā gharamā hunuhuncha?","तिम्रो बुबा घरमा हुनुहुन्छ?"
"353","Who is the oldest in your family?","tapāīṃko parivāramā sabaibhandā jeṭho ko ho?","तपाईंको परिवारमा सबैभन्दा जेठो को हो?"
"354","I grew up in a small town.","ma sāno śaharamā hurkieṃ.","म सानो शहरमा हुर्किएँ।"
"355","My parents met at university.","merā āmābābu viśvavidyālayamā bheṭekā thie.","मेरा आमाबाबु विश्वविद्यालयमा भेटेका थिए।"
"356","She was born in nineteen ninety.","unako janma unnāis say nabbemā bhaeko thiyo.","उनको जन्म उन्नाइस सय नब्बेमा भएको थियो।"
"357","We moved to this city five years ago.","hāmī pāṃc varṣaaghi yo śaharamā sarekā thiyauṃ.","हामी पाँच वर्षअघि यो शहरमा सरेका थियौं।"
"358","They got married last summer.","unīharūle gat garmīmā vivāh gare.","उनीहरूले गत गर्मीमा विवाह गरे।"
"359","My sister will have a baby next month.","merī bahinīle arko mahinā baccā janmāunechin.","मेरी बहिनीले अर्को महिना बच्चा जन्माउनेछिन्।"
"360","We will visit our grandparents this weekend.","hāmī yas saptāhantamā hāmrā hajurabā hajuraāmālāī bheṭna jānechauṃ.","हामी यस सप्ताहन्तमा हाम्रा हजुरबा हजुरआमालाई भेट्न जानेछौं।"
"361","He is going to move to a new apartment.","ū nayāṃ apārṭamenṭamā sardaich.","ऊ नयाँ अपार्टमेन्टमा सर्दैछ।"
"362","They will celebrate their anniversary.","unīharūle āphno vārṣikotsav manāunechan.","उनीहरूले आफ्नो वार्षिकोत्सव मनाउनेछन्।"
"363","If we had a bigger house, we would adopt a dog.","yadi hāmro ghar ṭhūlo bhaeko bhae, hāmī euṭā kukur pālne thiyauṃ.","यदि हाम्रो घर ठूलो भएको भए, हामी एउटा कुकुर पाल्ने थियौं।"
"364","If my grandparents were alive, they would be proud.","yadi merā hajurabā hajuraāmā jīvit bhaeko bhae, uhāṃharū garva garnuhunthyo.","यदि मेरा हजुरबा हजुरआमा जीवित भएको भए, उहाँहरू गर्व गर्नुहुन्थ्यो।"
"365","Children must respect their parents.","chorāchorīle āphnā āmābābuko ādar garnuparcha.","छोराछोरीले आफ्ना आमाबाबुको आदर गर्नुपर्छ।"
"366","You should call your mother.","timīle āphnī āmālāī phon garnuparcha.","तिमीले आफ्नी आमालाई फोन गर्नुपर्छ।"
"367","I can hear the baby crying.","ma baccā roeko sunna sakchu.","म बच्चा रोएको सुन्न सक्छु।"
"368","Close the door, please.","kr̥payā ḍhokā banda garnuhos.","कृपया ढोका बन्द गर्नुहोस्।"
"369","Do not make so much noise.","yati dherai hallā nagar.","यति धेरै हल्ला नगर।"
"370","Turn off the lights.","battīharū nibhāo.","बत्तीहरू निभाओ।"
"371","My house is bigger than yours.","mero ghar timro bhandā ṭhūlo cha.","मेरो घर तिम्रो भन्दा ठूलो छ।"
"372","She is taller than her brother.","unī āphno bhāibhandā aglī chin.","उनी आफ्नो भाइभन्दा अग्ली छिन्।"
"373","He is the youngest child in the family.","unī parivārako sabaibhandā kāncho santān hun.","उनी परिवारको सबैभन्दा कान्छो सन्तान हुन्।"
"374","This is the most beautiful garden I have ever seen.","yo maile dekheko sabaibhandā sundar bagaiṃcā ho.","यो मैले देखेको सबैभन्दा सुन्दर बगैंचा हो।"
"375","The children are raised by their grandparents.","baccāharūlāī unīharūkā hajurabā hajuraāmāle hurkāunuhuncha.","बच्चाहरूलाई उनीहरूका हजुरबा हजुरआमाले हुर्काउनुहुन्छ।"
"376","The dinner table was set by my sister.","khānāko ṭebal merī didīle milāin.","खानाको टेबल मेरी दिदीले मिलाइन्।"
"377","The woman who lives upstairs is my aunt.","māthillo talāmā basne mahilā merī kākī hun.","माथिल्लो तलामा बस्ने महिला मेरी काकी हुन्।"
"378","The house where I was born is very old.","ma janmeko ghar dherai purāno cha.","म जन्मेको घर धेरै पुरानो छ।"
"379","The children dress themselves.","baccāharūle āphaiṃ lugā lagāuṃchan.","बच्चाहरूले आफैँ लुगा लगाउँछन्।"
"380","My parents love each other.","merā āmābābu ekaarkālāī māyā garnuhuncha.","मेरा आमाबाबु एकअर्कालाई माया गर्नुहुन्छ।"
"381","I miss my family.","malāī mero parivārako yād āuṃch.","मलाई मेरो परिवारको याद आउँछ।"
"382","She looks like her mother.","unī āphnī āmā jastai dekhinchin.","उनी आफ्नी आमा जस्तै देखिन्छिन्।"
"383","He inherited the house from his uncle.","unale āphno kākābāṭ ghar virāsatamā pāekā thie.","उनले आफ्नो काकाबाट घर विरासतमा पाएका थिए।"
"384","We named the baby after my grandfather.","hāmīle baccāko nām mero hajurabubāko nāmabāṭ rākhyauṃ.","हामीले बच्चाको नाम मेरो हजुरबुबाको नामबाट राख्यौं।"
"385","Everyone in our family speaks two languages.","hāmro parivāramā sabaijanā duī bhāṣā bolchan.","हाम्रो परिवारमा सबैजना दुई भाषा बोल्छन्।"
"386","My home is where my family is.","mero ghar tyahī ho jahāṃ mero parivār cha.","मेरो घर त्यही हो जहाँ मेरो परिवार छ।"
"387","As a child, I used to play in the garden.","sāno chaṃdā ma bagaiṃcāmā kheltheṃ.","सानो छँदा म बगैंचामा खेल्थें।"
"388","The neighbors are very kind to us.","chimekīharū hāmīprati dherai dayālu chan.","छिमेकीहरू हामीप्रति धेरै दयालु छन्।"
"389","I work in an office.","ma euṭā kāryālayamā kām garchu.","म एउटा कार्यालयमा काम गर्छु।"
"390","She studies mathematics.","unī gaṇit paḍhchin.","उनी गणित पढ्छिन्।"
"391","He is a teacher.","uhāṃ śikṣak hunuhuncha.","उहाँ शिक्षक हुनुहुन्छ।"
"392","We have a meeting at three.","hāmro tīn baje baiṭhak cha.","हाम्रो तीन बजे बैठक छ।"
"393","They study together every day.","unīharū harek din saṃgai paḍhchan.","उनीहरू हरेक दिन सँगै पढ्छन्।"
"394","The students are in the classroom.","vidyārthīharū kakṣākoṭhāmā chan.","विद्यार्थीहरू कक्षाकोठामा छन्।"
"395","My boss is very strict.","mero hākim dherai kaḍā hunuhuncha.","मेरो हाकिम धेरै कडा हुनुहुन्छ।"
"396","She teaches English and French.","unī aṃgrejī ra phrenca bhāṣā paḍhāuṃchin.","उनी अंग्रेजी र फ्रेन्च भाषा पढाउँछिन्।"
"397","He writes reports every week.","unī harek haptā riporṭa lekhchan.","उनी हरेक हप्ता रिपोर्ट लेख्छन्।"
"398","I have an exam tomorrow.","bholi mero parīkṣā cha.","भोलि मेरो परीक्षा छ।"
"399","I started a new job last month.","maile gat mahinā nayāṃ kām suru gareṃ.","मैले गत महिना नयाँ काम सुरु गरें।"
"400","She graduated from university in June.","unale junamā viśvavidyālayabāṭ snātak garin.","उनले जुनमा विश्वविद्यालयबाट स्नातक गरिन्।"
"401","He was promoted to manager.","unalāī prabandhakamā padonnati gariyo.","उनलाई प्रबन्धकमा पदोन्नति गरियो।"
"402","We finished the project on time.","hāmīle samayamai pariyojanā pūrā garyauṃ.","हामीले समयमै परियोजना पूरा गर्यौं।"
"403","They did not pass the test.","unīharū parīkṣāmā uttīrṇa bhaenan.","उनीहरू परीक्षामा उत्तीर्ण भएनन्।"
"404","I will apply for the position.","ma padako lāgi āvedan dinechu.","म पदको लागि आवेदन दिनेछु।"
"405","She is going to start university.","unī viśvavidyālay suru gardaichin.","उनी विश्वविद्यालय सुरु गर्दैछिन्।"
"406","He will give a presentation next Monday.","uhāṃle arko somabār prastuti dinuhunech.","उहाँले अर्को सोमबार प्रस्तुति दिनुहुनेछ।"
"407","We will hire more employees.","hāmī thap karmacārīharū bharnā garnechauṃ.","हामी थप कर्मचारीहरू भर्ना गर्नेछौं।"
"408","Will you take the job?","ke tapāīṃ kām linuhuncha?","के तपाईं काम लिनुहुन्छ?"
"409","I am studying for an exam.","ma parīkṣāko lāgi paḍhdai chu.","म परीक्षाको लागि पढ्दै छु।"
"410","She is working on a new project.","unī nayāṃ pariyojanāmā kām garirahekī chin.","उनी नयाँ परियोजनामा काम गरिरहेकी छिन्।"
"411","They are having a meeting right now.","unīharū ahile baiṭhak gardaichan.","उनीहरू अहिले बैठक गर्दैछन्।"
"412","I have worked here for ten years.","ma yahāṃ daś varṣadekhi kām gardai āeko chu.","म यहाँ दश वर्षदेखि काम गर्दै आएको छु।"
"413","She has already finished her homework.","unale pahile nai āphno gr̥hakārya pūrā garisakekī chin.","उनले पहिले नै आफ्नो गृहकार्य पूरा गरिसकेकी छिन्।"
"414","He has been studying all day.","ū dinabhari paḍhiraheko cha.","ऊ दिनभरि पढिरहेको छ।"
"415","We had already started when the boss arrived.","hākim āipugdā hāmīle suru garisakekā thiyauṃ.","हाकिम आइपुग्दा हामीले सुरु गरिसकेका थियौं।"
"416","I do not understand this problem.","malāī yo samasyā bujhien.","मलाई यो समस्या बुझिएन।"
"417","She does not agree with the plan.","unī yojanāsaṃg sahamat chainan.","उनी योजनासँग सहमत छैनन्।"
"418","He cannot come to the meeting.","unī baiṭhakamā āun sakdainan.","उनी बैठकमा आउन सक्दैनन्।"
"419","What do you do for a living?","timī jīvan nirvāh garna ke kām garchau?","तिमी जीवन निर्वाह गर्न के काम गर्छौ?"
"420","Where does she work?","unī kahāṃ kām garchin?","उनी कहाँ काम गर्छिन्?"
"421","Did you finish the report?","ke tapāīṃle riporṭa pūrā garnubhayo?","के तपाईंले रिपोर्ट पूरा गर्नुभयो?"
"422","How was the exam?","parīkṣā kasto bhayo?","परीक्षा कस्तो भयो?"
"423","Who is your teacher?","timro guru ko ho?","तिम्रो गुरु को हो?"
"424","The homework was assigned yesterday.","gr̥hakārya hijo tokieko thiyo.","गृहकार्य हिजो तोकिएको थियो।"
"425","The results will be announced next week.","natijā arko haptā ghoṣaṇā garinech.","नतिजा अर्को हप्ता घोषणा गरिनेछ।"
"426","English is taught in many schools.","dherai vidyālayaharūmā aṃgrejī paḍhāincha.","धेरै विद्यालयहरूमा अंग्रेजी पढाइन्छ।"
"427","The report must be submitted by Friday.","prativedan śukrabārasamma bujhāisaknuparnech.","प्रतिवेदन शुक्रबारसम्म बुझाइसक्नुपर्नेछ।"
"428","If I study hard, I will pass the exam.","yadi maile kaḍā adhyayan gareṃ bhane, ma parīkṣā pās garnechu.","यदि मैले कडा अध्ययन गरें भने, म परीक्षा पास गर्नेछु।"
"429","If she had studied more, she would have passed.","yadi unale dherai paḍhekī bhae, unī pās hune thiin.","यदि उनले धेरै पढेकी भए, उनी पास हुने थिइन्।"
"430","You must finish your homework.","timīle āphno gr̥hakārya pūrā garnuparcha.","तिमीले आफ्नो गृहकार्य पूरा गर्नुपर्छ।"
"431","I should study harder.","maile ajh baḍhī adhyayan garnuparcha.","मैले अझ बढी अध्ययन गर्नुपर्छ।"
"432","She can speak three languages.","unī tīnavaṭā bhāṣā bolna sakchin.","उनी तीनवटा भाषा बोल्न सक्छिन्।"
"433","May I leave early today?","ke ma āj cāṃḍai jān sakchu?","के म आज चाँडै जान सक्छु?"
"434","Open your books to page ten.","āphno kitābako pr̥ṣṭha daś kholnuhos.","आफ्नो किताबको पृष्ठ दश खोल्नुहोस्।"
"435","Do not talk during the exam.","parīkṣāko samayamā kurā nagarnuhos.","परीक्षाको समयमा कुरा नगर्नुहोस्।"
"436","Study the first three chapters.","pahilo tīn adhyāyaharū adhyayan garnuhos.","पहिलो तीन अध्यायहरू अध्ययन गर्नुहोस्।"
"437","This problem is harder than the last one.","yo samasyā aghillo bhandā gāhro cha.","यो समस्या अघिल्लो भन्दा गाह्रो छ।"
"438","She is the best student in the class.","unī kakṣāko sabaibhandā rāmrī vidyārthī hun.","उनी कक्षाको सबैभन्दा राम्री विद्यार्थी हुन्।"
"439","Working from home is easier than going to the office.","gharabāṭ kām garnu aphis jānu bhandā sajilo cha.","घरबाट काम गर्नु अफिस जानु भन्दा सजिलो छ।"
"440","The teacher who helped me is retiring.","malāī sahayog garne śikṣak avakāś liṃdai hunuhuncha.","मलाई सहयोग गर्ने शिक्षक अवकाश लिँदै हुनुहुन्छ।"
"441","The school where I studied has closed.","maile paḍheko skūl banda bhaeko cha.","मैले पढेको स्कूल बन्द भएको छ।"
"442","I work hard and I study at night.","ma kaḍā pariśram garchu ra rātī paḍhchu.","म कडा परिश्रम गर्छु र राती पढ्छु।"
"443","She is tired but she keeps studying.","unī thākekī chin tar paḍhirahanchin.","उनी थाकेकी छिन् तर पढिरहन्छिन्।"
"444","He works because he needs money.","usalāī paisā cāhine bhaekole ū kām garcha.","उसलाई पैसा चाहिने भएकोले ऊ काम गर्छ।"
"445","My colleague is on vacation.","mero sahakarmī bidāmā cha.","मेरो सहकर्मी बिदामा छ।"
"446","The deadline is next Friday.","antim miti arko śukrabār ho.","अन्तिम मिति अर्को शुक्रबार हो।"
"447","I have too much work to do.","malāī dherai kām cha.","मलाई धेरै काम छ।"
"448","She earned a promotion.","unale padonnati pāin.","उनले पदोन्नति पाइन्।"
"449","The office is closed on weekends.","saptāhantamā kāryālay banda huncha.","सप्ताहन्तमा कार्यालय बन्द हुन्छ।"
"450","I applied for the scholarship.","maile chātravr̥ttiko lāgi āvedan dieṃ.","मैले छात्रवृत्तिको लागि आवेदन दिएँ।"
"451","Education is important for everyone.","śikṣā sabaiko lāgi mahattvapūrṇa cha.","शिक्षा सबैको लागि महत्त्वपूर्ण छ।"
"452","He was fired from his job.","unalāī jāgirabāṭ nikāliyo.","उनलाई जागिरबाट निकालियो।"
"453","I am looking for a new job.","ma nayāṃ jāgir khojdai chu.","म नयाँ जागिर खोज्दै छु।"
"454","What time does the class start?","kakṣā kati baje suru huncha?","कक्षा कति बजे सुरु हुन्छ?"
"455","The sun is shining.","ghām lāgiraheko cha.","घाम लागिरहेको छ।"
"456","It is raining.","pānī pariraheko cha.","पानी परिरहेको छ।"
"457","It is very cold today.","āj dherai jāḍo cha.","आज धेरै जाडो छ।"
"458","The sky is blue.","ākāś nīlo cha.","आकाश नीलो छ।"
"459","The wind is strong.","hāvā tej cha.","हावा तेज छ।"
"460","There are many trees in the forest.","jaṃgalamā dherai rūkhaharū chan.","जंगलमा धेरै रूखहरू छन्।"
"461","The river flows to the sea.","nadī samudramā bagcha.","नदी समुद्रमा बग्छ।"
"462","The mountains are covered with snow.","pahāḍaharū hiuṃle ḍhākiekā chan.","पहाडहरू हिउँले ढाकिएका छन्।"
"463","The flowers bloom in spring.","phūlaharū vasantamā phulchan.","फूलहरू वसन्तमा फुल्छन्।"
"464","The leaves fall in autumn.","pātaharū śarad r̥tumā jharchan.","पातहरू शरद ऋतुमा झर्छन्।"
"465","It rained all day yesterday.","hijo dinabhari pānī paryo.","हिजो दिनभरि पानी पर्यो।"
"466","The storm destroyed several houses.","āṃdhīle dherai gharaharū dhvasta pāreko cha.","आँधीले धेरै घरहरू ध्वस्त पारेको छ।"
"467","The lake was frozen last winter.","gat jāḍomā tāl jameko thiyo.","गत जाडोमा ताल जमेको थियो।"
"468","We saw a rainbow after the rain.","hāmīle pānī parepachi indreṇī dekhyauṃ.","हामीले पानी परेपछि इन्द्रेणी देख्यौं।"
"469","The temperature dropped below zero.","tāpakram śūnyabhandā tal jharyo.","तापक्रम शून्यभन्दा तल झर्यो।"
"470","It will snow tomorrow.","bholi hiuṃ parnech.","भोलि हिउँ पर्नेछ।"
"471","The weather will be warm next week.","arko haptā mausam tāto hunech.","अर्को हप्ता मौसम तातो हुनेछ।"
"472","Spring will come soon.","vasanta cāṃḍai āunech.","वसन्त चाँडै आउनेछ।"
"473","Will it rain this afternoon?","āj diuṃso pānī parnech?","आज दिउँसो पानी पर्नेछ?"
"474","It is not raining.","pānī pareko chain.","पानी परेको छैन।"
"475","The water is not clean.","pānī saphā chain.","पानी सफा छैन।"
"476","There are no clouds in the sky.","ākāśamā bādal chain.","आकाशमा बादल छैन।"
"477","What is the weather like today?","āj mausam kasto cha?","आज मौसम कस्तो छ?"
"478","Is it going to rain?","ke pānī parnech?","के पानी पर्नेछ?"
"479","How cold is it outside?","bāhir kati jāḍo cha?","बाहिर कति जाडो छ?"
"480","Where does this river lead?","yo nadī kahāṃ bagcha?","यो नदी कहाँ बग्छ?"
"481","Have you ever seen a volcano?","ke tapāīṃle kahilyai jvālāmukhī dekhnubhaeko cha?","के तपाईंले कहिल्यै ज्वालामुखी देख्नुभएको छ?"
"482","You should bring an umbrella.","timīle chātā lyāunu parcha.","तिमीले छाता ल्याउनु पर्छ।"
"483","It might rain later.","pachi pānī parna sakcha.","पछि पानी पर्न सक्छ।"
"484","We must protect the environment.","hāmīle vātāvaraṇ jogāunuparcha.","हामीले वातावरण जोगाउनुपर्छ।"
"485","Animals cannot survive without water.","pānī binā janāvaraharū bāṃcna sakdainan.","पानी बिना जनावरहरू बाँच्न सक्दैनन्।"
"486","The forest was destroyed by fire.","āgole jaṃgal naṣṭa bhayo.","आगोले जंगल नष्ट भयो।"
"487","The island is surrounded by water.","ṭāpu pānīle gherieko cha.","टापु पानीले घेरिएको छ।"
"488","Many animals are endangered.","dherai janāvaraharū saṃkaṭāpanna chan.","धेरै जनावरहरू संकटापन्न छन्।"
"489","Summer is hotter than spring.","garmī vasanta bhandā tāto huncha.","गर्मी वसन्त भन्दा तातो हुन्छ।"
"490","This is the tallest tree in the park.","yo pārkako sabaibhandā aglo rūkh ho.","यो पार्कको सबैभन्दा अग्लो रूख हो।"
"491","The ocean is deeper than any lake.","samundra kunai pani tāl bhandā gahiro cha.","समुन्द्र कुनै पनि ताल भन्दा गहिरो छ।"
"492","Mount Everest is the highest mountain in the world.","sagaramāthā saṃsārako sabaibhandā aglo himāl ho.","सगरमाथा संसारको सबैभन्दा अग्लो हिमाल हो।"
"493","If it rains, we will stay inside.","yadi pānī paryo bhane, hāmī bhitrai basnechauṃ.","यदि पानी पर्यो भने, हामी भित्रै बस्नेछौं।"
"494","If the ice melts, the sea level will rise.","yadi baraph pagliyo bhane, samudrako satah baḍhnech.","यदि बरफ पग्लियो भने, समुद्रको सतह बढ्नेछ।"
"495","If we do not protect nature, many species will disappear.","yadi hāmīle prakr̥tilāī saṃrakṣaṇ garenauṃ bhane, dherai prajātiharū lop hunechan.","यदि हामीले प्रकृतिलाई संरक्षण गरेनौं भने, धेरै प्रजातिहरू लोप हुनेछन्।"
"496","The sun sets and the moon rises.","sūrya astāuṃch ra candramā udāuṃch.","सूर्य अस्ताउँछ र चन्द्रमा उदाउँछ।"
"497","It is cold but sunny.","ciso cha tar ghām lāgeko cha.","चिसो छ तर घाम लागेको छ।"
"498","I like summer because the days are long.","malāī garmī man parcha kinabhane dinaharū lāmo hunchan.","मलाई गर्मी मन पर्छ किनभने दिनहरू लामो हुन्छन्।"
"499","The mountain that we climbed was very high.","hāmīle caḍheko pahāḍ dherai aglo thiyo.","हामीले चढेको पहाड धेरै अग्लो थियो।"
"500","The river where we swam was crystal clear.","hāmīle pauḍī kheleko nadī krisṭal saphā thiyo.","हामीले पौडी खेलेको नदी क्रिस्टल सफा थियो।"
"501","The air smells fresh after the rain.","pānī parepachi hāvāmā tājā gandha āuṃch.","पानी परेपछि हावामा ताजा गन्ध आउँछ।"
"502","Birds sing in the morning.","bihān carāharūle gāuṃchan.","बिहान चराहरूले गाउँछन्।"
"503","The garden is full of flowers.","bagaiṃcā phūlaharūle bharieko cha.","बगैंचा फूलहरूले भरिएको छ।"
"504","A strong earthquake shook the city.","euṭā śaktiśālī bhūkampale śahar hallāyo.","एउटा शक्तिशाली भूकम्पले शहर हल्लायो।"
"505","The sunset was beautiful.","sūryāsta sundar thiyo.","सूर्यास्त सुन्दर थियो।"
"506","Snow covers the ground in winter.","jāḍomā jamin hiuṃle ḍhākcha.","जाडोमा जमिन हिउँले ढाक्छ।"
"507","The weather has been very hot this week.","yo haptā mausam dherai garmī bhaeko cha.","यो हप्ता मौसम धेरै गर्मी भएको छ।"
"508","It hardly ever snows here.","yahāṃ muśkilale hiuṃ parcha.","यहाँ मुश्किलले हिउँ पर्छ।"
"509","Lightning struck the old tree.","purāno rūkhamā caṭyāṅ paryo.","पुरानो रूखमा चट्याङ पर्यो।"
"510","The tide comes in twice a day.","jvārabhāṭā dinamā duī paṭak āuṃch.","ज्वारभाटा दिनमा दुई पटक आउँछ।"
"511","I have a headache.","malāī ṭāuko dukheko cha.","मलाई टाउको दुखेको छ।"
"512","She is sick.","unī birāmī chin.","उनी बिरामी छिन्।"
"513","He broke his arm.","usako hāt bhāṃciyo.","उसको हात भाँचियो।"
"514","My stomach hurts.","mero peṭ dukhcha.","मेरो पेट दुख्छ।"
"515","The doctor examined the patient.","ḍākṭarale birāmīko jāṃc gare.","डाक्टरले बिरामीको जाँच गरे।"
"516","We need to go to the hospital.","hāmīlāī aspatāl jānuparcha.","हामीलाई अस्पताल जानुपर्छ।"
"517","They gave him medicine.","unīharūle usalāī auṣadhi die.","उनीहरूले उसलाई औषधि दिए।"
"518","I feel much better today.","āj malāī dherai rāmro mahasus bhairaheko cha.","आज मलाई धेरै राम्रो महसुस भइरहेको छ।"
"519","She takes medicine every day.","unī harek din auṣadhi khānchin.","उनी हरेक दिन औषधि खान्छिन्।"
"520","He cannot see without his glasses.","ū casmā binā dekhna sakdain.","ऊ चस्मा बिना देख्न सक्दैन।"
"521","She has long hair.","unako kapāl lāmo cha.","उनको कपाल लामो छ।"
"522","His eyes are brown.","usakā āṃkhā khairo chan.","उसका आँखा खैरो छन्।"
"523","My hands are cold.","merā hātaharū ciso chan.","मेरा हातहरू चिसो छन्।"
"524","The child cut his finger.","baccāle āphno auṃlā kāṭyo.","बच्चाले आफ्नो औंला काट्यो।"
"525","I can hear with my ears.","ma mero kānale sunna sakchu.","म मेरो कानले सुन्न सक्छु।"
"526","I do not feel well.","malāī sanco chain.","मलाई सन्चो छैन।"
"527","She is not allergic to anything.","unalāī kunai pani kurābāṭ elarjī chain.","उनलाई कुनै पनि कुराबाट एलर्जी छैन।"
"528","He did not take his medicine.","unale āphno auṣadhi khāenan.","उनले आफ्नो औषधि खाएनन्।"
"529","How do you feel?","timīlāī kasto lāgcha?","तिमीलाई कस्तो लाग्छ?"
"530","Does your head hurt?","ke tapāīṃko ṭāuko dukhcha?","के तपाईंको टाउको दुख्छ?"
"531","What happened to your hand?","timro hāt ke bhayo?","तिम्रो हात के भयो?"
"532","Have you been to the doctor?","ke tapāīṃ ḍākṭarakahāṃ jānubhaeko cha?","के तपाईं डाक्टरकहाँ जानुभएको छ?"
"533","When should I take the medicine?","maile auṣadhi kahile khānuparcha?","मैले औषधि कहिले खानुपर्छ?"
"534","I will see the doctor tomorrow.","ma bholi ḍākṭaralāī bheṭnechu.","म भोलि डाक्टरलाई भेट्नेछु।"
"535","She will recover soon.","unī cāṃḍai niko hunechin.","उनी चाँडै निको हुनेछिन्।"
"536","The operation will be performed next week.","śalyakriyā arko haptā garinech.","शल्यक्रिया अर्को हप्ता गरिनेछ।"
"537","I have had a cold for three days.","malāī tīn dinadekhi rughā lāgeko cha.","मलाई तीन दिनदेखि रुघा लागेको छ।"
"538","She has never been to the hospital.","unī kahilyai aspatāl gaekī chainan.","उनी कहिल्यै अस्पताल गएकी छैनन्।"
"539","He has already recovered.","unī pahile nai niko bhaisakekā chan.","उनी पहिले नै निको भइसकेका छन्।"
"540","The patient was treated by a specialist.","birāmīko upacār viśeṣajñadvārā gariyo.","बिरामीको उपचार विशेषज्ञद्वारा गरियो।"
"541","The medicine must be taken twice a day.","auṣadhi dinamā duī paṭak khānuparcha.","औषधि दिनमा दुई पटक खानुपर्छ।"
"542","You should rest.","timī ārām garnu parcha.","तिमी आराम गर्नु पर्छ।"

"544","She can walk again after the surgery.","śalyakriyā pachi unī pheri hiṃḍna sakchin.","शल्यक्रिया पछि उनी फेरि हिँड्न सक्छिन्।"
"545","He might need an operation.","usalāī śalyakriyā āvaśyak parna sakcha.","उसलाई शल्यक्रिया आवश्यक पर्न सक्छ।"
"546","Drink plenty of water.","praśasta pānī piunuhos.","प्रशस्त पानी पिउनुहोस्।"
"547","Do not eat too much sugar.","dherai cinī nakhānuhos.","धेरै चिनी नखानुहोस्।"
"548","Take this medicine after meals.","khānā pachi yo auṣadhi linuhos.","खाना पछि यो औषधि लिनुहोस्।"
"549","If you exercise regularly, you will be healthier.","yadi tapāīṃle niyamit vyāyām garnubhayo bhane, tapāīṃ svastha hunuhunech.","यदि तपाईंले नियमित व्यायाम गर्नुभयो भने, तपाईं स्वस्थ हुनुहुनेछ।"
"550","If I had slept more, I would not be so tired.","yadi ma dherai suteko bhae, ma yati thākeko hune thiinaṃ.","यदि म धेरै सुतेको भए, म यति थाकेको हुने थिइनँ।"
"551","I feel better than yesterday.","malāī hijo bhandā rāmro mahasus bhairaheko cha.","मलाई हिजो भन्दा राम्रो महसुस भइरहेको छ।"

"554","Exercise is good for your health.","vyāyām tapāīṃko svāsthyako lāgi rāmro cha.","व्यायाम तपाईंको स्वास्थ्यको लागि राम्रो छ।"

"559","She was born with a heart condition.","unī janmajāt muṭuko rog lier janmiekī thiin.","उनी जन्मजात मुटुको रोग लिएर जन्मिएकी थिइन्।"
"560","He hurt himself while playing.","kheldā kheldai usale āphailāī coṭ puryāyo.","खेल्दा खेल्दै उसले आफैलाई चोट पुर्यायो।"

"760","Have they eaten dinner yet?","ke unīharūle khānā khāekā chan?","के उनीहरूले खाना खाएका छन्?"

"762","We could hear the rain from inside.","hāmīle bhitrabāṭ pānī pareko sunna sakyauṃ.","हामीले भित्रबाट पानी परेको सुन्न सक्यौं।"
"763","If you go to bed late, you will be tired.","yadi tapāīṃ ḍhilo sutnubhayo bhane, tapāīṃ thākeko mahasus garnuhunech.","यदि तपाईं ढिलो सुत्नुभयो भने, तपाईं थाकेको महसुस गर्नुहुनेछ।"
"764","Do not leave the lights on.","battīharū bāler nachoḍnuhos.","बत्तीहरू बालेर नछोड्नुहोस्।"
"765","I make my bed every morning.","ma harek bihān mero ochyān banāuṃchu.","म हरेक बिहान मेरो ओछ्यान बनाउँछु।"
"766","The laundry has been done.","lugā dhune kām sakiyo.","लुगा धुने काम सकियो।"
"767","Nobody answered the phone.","kasaile pani phon uṭhāenan.","कसैले पनि फोन उठाएनन्।"
"768","Either you clean up or I will.","yā ta timī saphā gar, natra ma saphā garchu.","या त तिमी सफा गर, नत्र म सफा गर्छु।"
"769","Whose shoes are these?","yī kasakā juttā hun?","यी कसका जुत्ता हुन्?"
"770","These are not mine.","yī merā hoinan.","यी मेरा होइनन्।"
"771","Each of us has a task.","hāmī pratyekako euṭā kām cha.","हामी प्रत्येकको एउटा काम छ।"
"772","All of the rooms have been cleaned.","sabai koṭhāharū saphā gariekā chan.","सबै कोठाहरू सफा गरिएका छन्।"
"773","I myself cook dinner on Fridays.","śukrabārako khānā ma āphaiṃ pakāuṃchu.","शुक्रबारको खाना म आफैं पकाउँछु।"
"774","You cooked a wonderful meal.","timīle mīṭho khānā pakāyau.","तिमीले मीठो खाना पकायौ।"
"775","We are eating lunch.","hāmī khājā khāirahekā chauṃ.","हामी खाजा खाइरहेका छौं।"
"776","Were they cooking when you arrived?","timī āipugdā ke unīharū khānā pakāuṃdai thie?","तिमी आइपुग्दा के उनीहरू खाना पकाउँदै थिए?"
"777","Will the food be ready by seven?","ke khānā sāt bajesammamā tayār huncha?","के खाना सात बजेसम्ममा तयार हुन्छ?"
"778","The cake is being baked right now.","kek ahile bek huṃdaich.","केक अहिले बेक हुँदैछ।"
"779","You should not eat so fast.","timīle yati chiṭo khānā khānu huṃdain.","तिमीले यति छिटो खाना खानु हुँदैन।"
"780","If she had added more salt, it would have tasted better.","yadi unale ajhai nun thapekī bhae, yasako svād ajh rāmro hune thiyo.","यदि उनले अझै नुन थपेकी भए, यसको स्वाद अझ राम्रो हुने थियो।"
"781","I ate neither the soup nor the salad.","maile na ta sup khāeṃ na ta salād.","मैले न त सुप खाएँ न त सलाद।"
"782","Each plate has a different design.","pratyek pleṭako pharak ḍijāin huncha.","प्रत्येक प्लेटको फरक डिजाइन हुन्छ।"
"783","These apples are sweeter than those.","yī syāuharū tī bhandā guliyo chan.","यी स्याउहरू ती भन्दा गुलियो छन्।"
"784","What kind of food do they eat?","unīharū kasto prakārako khānā khānchan?","उनीहरू कस्तो प्रकारको खाना खान्छन्?"
"785","We ourselves grew these vegetables.","hāmī āphaiṃle yī tarakārīharū ubjāyauṃ.","हामी आफैंले यी तरकारीहरू उब्जायौं।"
"786","Whose plate is this?","yo kasako pleṭ ho?","यो कसको प्लेट हो?"
"787","It smells wonderful in here.","yahāṃ gandha adbhut cha.","यहाँ गन्ध अद्भुत छ।"
"788","The more you cook, the better you get.","jati dherai pakāunuhuncha, tyati nai rāmro pāunuhuncha.","जति धेरै पकाउनुहुन्छ, त्यति नै राम्रो पाउनुहुन्छ।"
"789","I would rather have tea than coffee.","malāī kaphī bhandā ciyā nai man parcha.","मलाई कफी भन्दा चिया नै मन पर्छ।"
"790","Somebody left the oven on.","kasaile culho bāler rākhyo.","कसैले चुल्हो बालेर राख्यो।"
"791","Has anyone tried this dessert?","ke kasaile yo miṭhāī prayās garnubhaeko cha?","के कसैले यो मिठाई प्रयास गर्नुभएको छ?"
"792","Nothing tastes better than homemade bread.","gharamai banāeko roṭī bhandā rāmro arū kehī huṃdain.","घरमै बनाएको रोटी भन्दा राम्रो अरू केही हुँदैन।"
"793","You had better not eat that.","timīle tyo nakhāekai rāmro.","तिमीले त्यो नखाएकै राम्रो।"
"794","The recipe calls for two cups of flour.","yo resipīmā duī kap pīṭho cāhincha.","यो रेसिपीमा दुई कप पीठो चाहिन्छ।"
"795","We seldom eat out.","hāmī bāhir viralai khānchauṃ.","हामी बाहिर विरलै खान्छौं।"
"796","I will have cooked by the time you arrive.","timī āipugdāsamma ma khānā pakāisakeko hunechu.","तिमी आइपुग्दासम्म म खाना पकाइसकेको हुनेछु।"
"797","You traveled by yourself.","timī āphaiṃ yātrā garyau.","तिमी आफैं यात्रा गर्यौ।"
"798","We have been traveling for a week.","hāmī ek haptādekhi yātrā garirahekā chauṃ.","हामी एक हप्तादेखि यात्रा गरिरहेका छौं।"
"799","The passengers were asked to remain seated.","yātruharūlāī basna bhaniyo.","यात्रुहरूलाई बस्न भनियो।"
"800","I will have arrived by noon.","ma diuṃsosamma āipugnechu.","म दिउँसोसम्म आइपुग्नेछु।"
"801","Were you waiting long?","ke tapāīṃ lāmo samayadekhi parkhirahanubhaeko thiyo?","के तपाईं लामो समयदेखि पर्खिरहनुभएको थियो?"
"802","She herself carried all the bags.","unī āphaiṃle sabai jholāharū bokin.","उनी आफैंले सबै झोलाहरू बोकिन्।"
"803","Nobody told us about the delay.","ḍhilāiko bāremā hāmīlāī kasaile bhanen.","ढिलाइको बारेमा हामीलाई कसैले भनेन।"
"804","Whose suitcase is this?","yo kasako suṭakes ho?","यो कसको सुटकेस हो?"
"805","The train had left before we arrived.","hāmī āipugnubhandā pahile nai rel chuṭisakeko thiyo.","हामी आइपुग्नुभन्दा पहिले नै रेल छुटिसकेको थियो।"
"806","Each passenger must show identification.","pratyek yātrule paricayapatra dekhāunu parnech.","प्रत्येक यात्रुले परिचयपत्र देखाउनु पर्नेछ।"
"807","I would rather fly than take the bus.","ma bas caḍhnubhandā uḍer jān rucāuṃchu.","म बस चढ्नुभन्दा उडेर जान रुचाउँछु।"
"808","You must not take photographs here.","yahāṃ phoṭo khicnu huṃdain.","यहाँ फोटो खिच्नु हुँदैन।"
"809","The road was being repaired.","bāṭo marmat bhairaheko thiyo.","बाटो मर्मत भइरहेको थियो।"
"810","Neither the bus nor the train was on time.","na ta bas na ta rel samayamai thiyo.","न त बस न त रेल समयमै थियो।"
"811","Which platform does the train leave from?","rel kun pleṭapharmabāṭ chuṭcha?","रेल कुन प्लेटफर्मबाट छुट्छ?"
"812","Either we walk or we take a taxi.","yā ta hāmī hiṃḍer jānchauṃ yā ṭyāksī caḍhchauṃ.","या त हामी हिँडेर जान्छौं या ट्याक्सी चढ्छौं।"
"813","He had never flown before that trip.","tyo yātrā aghi unī kahilyai uḍekā thienan.","त्यो यात्रा अघि उनी कहिल्यै उडेका थिएनन्।"
"814","How far is the airport from here?","yahāṃbāṭ vimānasthal kati ṭāḍhā cha?","यहाँबाट विमानस्थल कति टाढा छ?"
"815","The sooner we leave, the earlier we arrive.","hāmī jati cāṃḍo niskanchauṃ, tyati nai cāṃḍo āipugchauṃ.","हामी जति चाँडो निस्कन्छौं, त्यति नै चाँडो आइपुग्छौं।"
"816","I was told to wait here.","malāī yahāṃ parkhan bhanieko thiyo.","मलाई यहाँ पर्खन भनिएको थियो।"
"817","You have a beautiful family.","timro parivār sundar cha.","तिम्रो परिवार सुन्दर छ।"
"818","They are building a new house.","unīharū nayāṃ ghar banāuṃdaichan.","उनीहरू नयाँ घर बनाउँदैछन्।"
"819","Were your parents born in this country?","ke tapāīṃkā āmābābu yahī deśamā janmiekā hun?","के तपाईंका आमाबाबु यही देशमा जन्मिएका हुन्?"
"820","I will have lived here for twenty years by then.","tyatinjel ma yahāṃ bīs varṣa basisakeko hunechu.","त्यतिन्जेल म यहाँ बीस वर्ष बसिसकेको हुनेछु।"
"821","Nobody in our family smokes.","hāmro parivāramā kohī pani dhūmrapān gardainan.","हाम्रो परिवारमा कोही पनि धूम्रपान गर्दैनन्।"
"822","Both my parents work.","merā āmābābu duvai kām garnuhuncha.","मेरा आमाबाबु दुवै काम गर्नुहुन्छ।"
"823","Neither my brother nor my sister lives nearby.","mero bhāi na ta bahinī najikai baschan.","मेरो भाइ न त बहिनी नजिकै बस्छन्।"
"824","Each child has their own room.","pratyek baccāko āphnai koṭhā huncha.","प्रत्येक बच्चाको आफ्नै कोठा हुन्छ।"
"825","Whose toys are these?","yī kasakā khelaunā hun?","यी कसका खेलौना हुन्?"
"826","It was my grandmother who taught me to cook.","malāī khānā pakāun sikāunuhune merī hajuraāmā hunuhunthyo.","मलाई खाना पकाउन सिकाउनुहुने मेरी हजुरआमा हुनुहुन्थ्यो।"
"827","My parents had already gone to bed when I came home.","ma ghar āuṃdā merā āmābābu sutna jānubhaeko thiyo.","म घर आउँदा मेरा आमाबाबु सुत्न जानुभएको थियो।"
"828","We ourselves built this fence.","yo bār hāmīle āphaiṃle banāekā hauṃ.","यो बार हामीले आफैंले बनाएका हौं।"
"829","He is taller than any of his brothers.","ū āphnā kunai pani dājubhāibhandā aglo cha.","ऊ आफ्ना कुनै पनि दाजुभाइभन्दा अग्लो छ।"
"830","I am the only child in my family.","ma mero parivārako eklo santān huṃ.","म मेरो परिवारको एक्लो सन्तान हुँ।"
"831","You should spend more time with your family.","tapāīṃle āphno parivārasaṃg baḍhī samay bitāunu parcha.","तपाईंले आफ्नो परिवारसँग बढी समय बिताउनु पर्छ।"
"832","Had they moved before the baby was born?","ke tinīharū baccā janmanubhandā pahile nai sarekā thie?","के तिनीहरू बच्चा जन्मनुभन्दा पहिले नै सरेका थिए?"
"833","The house is being renovated.","gharako marmatasambhār bhairaheko cha.","घरको मर्मतसम्भार भइरहेको छ।"
"834","I wish my family lived closer.","ma cāhanchu ki mero parivār najikai basos.","म चाहन्छु कि मेरो परिवार नजिकै बसोस्।"
"835","It is my mother who cooks the best meals.","sabaibhandā rāmro khānā pakāune merī āmā nai hunuhuncha.","सबैभन्दा राम्रो खाना पकाउने मेरी आमा नै हुनुहुन्छ।"
"836","You need to study harder.","timīle ajh kaḍā adhyayan garnuparcha.","तिमीले अझ कडा अध्ययन गर्नुपर्छ।"
"837","We are being trained by the manager.","hāmīlāī prabandhakale tālim diirahekā chan.","हामीलाई प्रबन्धकले तालिम दिइरहेका छन्।"
"838","Had she finished the project before the deadline?","ke unale samayasīmā agāḍi nai pariyojanā pūrā garin?","के उनले समयसीमा अगाडि नै परियोजना पूरा गरिन्?"
"839","The exam will have been graded by Monday.","somabārasammamā parīkṣāko greḍiṅ bhaisaknech.","सोमबारसम्ममा परीक्षाको ग्रेडिङ भइसक्नेछ।"
"840","Neither the teacher nor the students understood.","na śikṣakale bujhe na vidyārthīharūle nai.","न शिक्षकले बुझे न विद्यार्थीहरूले नै।"
"841","Each student must write an essay.","pratyek vidyārthīle nibandha lekhnu parcha.","प्रत्येक विद्यार्थीले निबन्ध लेख्नु पर्छ।"
"842","Whose pen is this?","yo kasako kalam ho?","यो कसको कलम हो?"
"843","It was the teacher who explained the problem.","samasyā vyākhyā garne śikṣak nai thie.","समस्या व्याख्या गर्ने शिक्षक नै थिए।"
"844","I myself completed the entire project.","maile āphaiṃle sampūrṇa pariyojanā pūrā gareṃ.","मैले आफैंले सम्पूर्ण परियोजना पूरा गरें।"
"845","Nobody passed the exam.","kohī pani parīkṣā pās bhaenan.","कोही पनि परीक्षा पास भएनन्।"
"846","Were you working when I called?","maile phon gardā timī kāmamā thiyau?","मैले फोन गर्दा तिमी काममा थियौ?"
"847","Both the students and the teacher are responsible.","vidyārthī ra śikṣak duvai jimmevār chan.","विद्यार्थी र शिक्षक दुवै जिम्मेवार छन्।"
"848","You had better finish your work.","timīle āphno kām sidhyāunu rāmro huncha.","तिमीले आफ्नो काम सिध्याउनु राम्रो हुन्छ।"
"849","Either she resigns or she is fired.","yā ta unale rājīnāmā dinchin yā ta unalāī barkhāsta garincha.","या त उनले राजीनामा दिन्छिन् या त उनलाई बर्खास्त गरिन्छ।"
"850","The harder you study, the higher your grades will be.","tapāīṃ jati mehanat garnuhuncha, tapāīṃko greḍ tyati nai ucca hunech.","तपाईं जति मेहनत गर्नुहुन्छ, तपाईंको ग्रेड त्यति नै उच्च हुनेछ।"
"851","I would rather work from home.","ma gharabāṭai kām garna cāhanchu.","म घरबाटै काम गर्न चाहन्छु।"
"852","I have not been given a raise.","malāī talab baḍhāieko chain.","मलाई तलब बढाइएको छैन।"

"854","Whatever you decide, I will support you.","timīle je nirṇay garyau, ma timīlāī sāth dinechu.","तिमीले जे निर्णय गर्यौ, म तिमीलाई साथ दिनेछु।"
"855","She asked whether the meeting had been canceled.","unale baiṭhak radda bhaeko ho ki bhaner sodhin.","उनले बैठक रद्द भएको हो कि भनेर सोधिन्।"
"856","You saw the eclipse.","tapāīṃle grahaṇ dekhnubhayo.","तपाईंले ग्रहण देख्नुभयो।"
"857","We have been watching the storm.","hāmī āṃdhībeharī herirahekā chauṃ.","हामी आँधीबेहरी हेरिरहेका छौं।"
"858","Were the animals frightened by the thunder?","ke janāvaraharū garjanabāṭ ḍarāe?","के जनावरहरू गर्जनबाट डराए?"
"859","The crops will have been harvested by October.","akṭobarasammamā bālī kāṭisakeko hunech.","अक्टोबरसम्ममा बाली काटिसकेको हुनेछ।"
"860","Neither the heat nor the cold bothers me.","na ta garmīle na ta cisole malāī satāuṃch.","न त गर्मीले न त चिसोले मलाई सताउँछ।"
"861","Each season has its own beauty.","harek r̥tuko āphnai sundaratā huncha.","हरेक ऋतुको आफ्नै सुन्दरता हुन्छ।"
"862","Whose land is this?","yo kasako jaggā ho?","यो कसको जग्गा हो?"
"863","It was the earthquake that destroyed the bridge.","bhūkampale pul bhatkāyo.","भूकम्पले पुल भत्कायो।"
"864","We ourselves planted these trees.","yī rūkhaharū hāmīle āphaiṃle ropekā hauṃ.","यी रूखहरू हामीले आफैंले रोपेका हौं।"
"865","Nobody expected the flood.","kasaile pani bāḍhīko āśā garekā thienan.","कसैले पनि बाढीको आशा गरेका थिएनन्।"
"866","Both the river and the lake are polluted.","nadī ra tāl duvai pradūṣit chan.","नदी र ताल दुवै प्रदूषित छन्।"
"867","Either it rains or it stays dry.","yā ta pānī parcha yā ta sukhkhā rahancha.","या त पानी पर्छ या त सुख्खा रहन्छ।"
"868","The longer it rains, the higher the river rises.","jati lāmo pānī parcha, nadīko pānī tyati nai māthi uṭhcha.","जति लामो पानी पर्छ, नदीको पानी त्यति नै माथि उठ्छ।"
"869","I would rather have sunshine.","malāī ghām lāgcha.","मलाई घाम लाग्छ।"
"870","You had better bring warm clothes.","timīle nyāno lugā lier jānu rāmro huncha.","तिमीले न्यानो लुगा लिएर जानु राम्रो हुन्छ।"
"871","Whatever happens, nature will recover.","je bhae pani, prakr̥ti pheri ṭhīk hunech.","जे भए पनि, प्रकृति फेरि ठीक हुनेछ।"
"872","The forest has been burning for days.","jaṃgal dherai dinadekhi jaliraheko cha.","जंगल धेरै दिनदेखि जलिरहेको छ।"
"873","Had the volcano erupted before?","ke pahile pani jvālāmukhī visphoṭ bhaeko thiyo?","के पहिले पनि ज्वालामुखी विस्फोट भएको थियो?"
"874","It is being investigated whether the water is safe.","pānī surakṣit cha ki chain bhaner anusandhān bhairaheko cha.","पानी सुरक्षित छ कि छैन भनेर अनुसन्धान भइरहेको छ।"

"988","They promised to help us.","unīharūle hāmīlāī sahayog garne vācā gare.","उनीहरूले हामीलाई सहयोग गर्ने वाचा गरे।"
"989","I do not understand what he is saying.","uhāṃle ke bhanirahanubhaeko cha malāī bujhien.","उहाँले के भनिरहनुभएको छ मलाई बुझिएन।"

"1003","I was talking to her when you interrupted.","timīle bādhā diṃdā ma unīsaṃg kurā gariraheko thieṃ.","तिमीले बाधा दिँदा म उनीसँग कुरा गरिरहेको थिएँ।"
"1004","She is explaining the rules.","unī niyamaharū vyākhyā gardaichin.","उनी नियमहरू व्याख्या गर्दैछिन्।"

"1020","If I had known your number, I would have called you.","yadi malāī timro nambar thāhā bhaeko bhae, ma timīlāī phon gartheṃ.","यदि मलाई तिम्रो नम्बर थाहा भएको भए, म तिमीलाई फोन गर्थें।"
"1021","If she were here, she would agree.","yadi unī yahāṃ bhaeko bhae, unī sahamat hunthin.","यदि उनी यहाँ भएको भए, उनी सहमत हुन्थिन्।"

"1424","You should have seen the sunset.","timīle sūryāsta dekheko hunuparcha.","तिमीले सूर्यास्त देखेको हुनुपर्छ।"

"1427","If we had bought tickets earlier, we would have had better seats.","yadi hāmīle pahile nai ṭikaṭ kineko bhae, hāmīsaṃg rāmro siṭ hune thiyo.","यदि हामीले पहिले नै टिकट किनेको भए, हामीसँग राम्रो सिट हुने थियो।"
